# Migration board

The users-branch tables imported below are historical merge evidence, not a new
integration run. `board.json` remains the pre-users integration snapshot unchanged;
it has not been regenerated. Current harness rules retain every LOGIN_EVENT outcome,
retire the four owner-approved preference objects while keeping the Spark PREF_JSON
feed, and exclude the disabled TEAM_MEMBER session from folder-wide builds.

## Parity by subject area

An object counts as green only when its DuckDB column is green.
Snowflake login down; execution disabled.

| Subject area | DuckDB | Snowflake |
|---|---:|---:|
| **Total** | 74 green / 168 red / 4 retire_verified / 6 approved_deviation | 0 green / 252 red |
| audit | 0 green / 57 red | 0 green / 57 red |
| billing | 29 green / 45 red | 0 green / 74 red |
| content | 0 green / 1 red | 0 green / 1 red |
| dbc | 0 green / 6 red | 0 green / 6 red |
| documents | 0 green / 59 red | 0 green / 59 red |
| users | 45 green / 0 red / 4 retire_verified / 6 approved_deviation | 0 green / 55 red |

## DBC parity provenance

Local ACCOUNT_USAGE shapes are assumed from identical DBQL CSVs; live ACCOUNT_USAGE is unverified (Snowflake login down; no connection attempted (sfg1120)). Owner approved missing CPU/IO/client-id/ProcID, collection/access timestamps, M/P EXECUTE attribution and M/P catalog omissions. StatementGroup and all other differences stay red. This is not catalog deletion approval.
Mapped-columns ladders exclude the named decision columns **and explicitly labelled unsupported row kinds**; their green result is not full-shape parity.

### DBC.DBC_QRYLOG

Differences outside approved missing-field scope; see owner_decision.approved_comparison; StatementGroup comparison is not owner approved
Mapped scope: `all rows`; columns: `QueryID, UserName, DefaultDatabase, AppID, StartTime, FirstRespTime, StatementType, QueryText, NumResultRows`.
Excluded decisions: ProcID, CollectTimeStamp, ClientID, StatementGroup, AMPCPUTime, TotalIOCount, ErrorCode.


### DBC.DBC_QRYLOGOBJECTS

Owner-approved Snowflake semantics: unavailable CPU, IO, client-id and ProcID are NULL; collection and last-access timestamps may differ; macro/procedure EXECUTE attribution and M/P catalog rows may be absent. StatementGroup and every other retained field and row multiplicity must match.
Mapped scope: ` WHERE UsageType IN ('READ','WRITE') AND ObjectType IN ('T','V')`; columns: `QueryID, ObjectDatabaseName, ObjectTableName, ObjectType, UsageType`.
Excluded decisions: EXECUTE usages and M/P kinds: no established equivalent.


### DBC.DBC_TABLESV

Owner-approved Snowflake semantics: unavailable CPU, IO, client-id and ProcID are NULL; collection and last-access timestamps may differ; macro/procedure EXECUTE attribution and M/P catalog rows may be absent. StatementGroup and every other retained field and row multiplicity must match.
Mapped scope: ` WHERE TableKind IN ('T','V')`; columns: `DatabaseName, TableName, TableKind, CreateTimeStamp`.
Excluded decisions: AccessCount, LastAccessTimeStamp, M/P catalog rows.
